```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""§P-KV-1 — addressed content without the tape (KV splice, no tokens, no weights).

Drafted s295 (Michael GO "yes let's try P-KV-1"). The register fork: transient
unaddressed injection fails (P-STACK-1b/3a/P-ENRICH-1) while tape-addressed
content works (CoT 9/10, scaffold 10/10). A KV-cache entry is ALSO
tape-addressed — content at a RoPE position, attendable, never generated.
Deliver the intermediate (country) in the ADDRESSED register and ask whether
resident hop-2 (country->capital) completes one-shot.

Implementation: ONE forward per cell-arm — donor segment + test segment with an
additive 4D attention mask. Donor rows: plain causal (never see the test). Test
rows: causal within test + donor position 0 (sink parity, ALL arms) + the
selected donor columns only. Donors are padded to a fixed length so test-token
RoPE positions are identical across arms (padding appended AFTER the selected
columns; causal encoding leaves their KV untouched). Runtime self-check: the
4D mask path must reproduce plain-forward logits (abort otherwise — no verdict
from an unverified mask path).

Arms: base / kv_nat / kv_wrong / kv_rand / kv_synth / resid (see pre-reg).
kv_synth vs resid = the REGISTER FORK: identical injected content (d_ct @ L_e),
addressed vs unaddressed. Gates: G1 kv_nat>base + flip (primary); G2
specificity + SWAP flag; G3 vs kv_rand; G4 register fork (mechanism clause,
never decides the headline); secondary operand-error shift. Verdicts:
ADDRESSED-COMPOSES (+ADDRESS-SUFFICIENT | +RE-ENCODING-REQUIRED) / KV-PRIMING /
ANY-KV-ARTIFACT / ADDRESS-FAILS.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(_HERE) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HERE))

# reuse (no fork): chain data + prompts + classifier + dsp
from fn_stack import COUNTRY_CAP, NONCE_PROMPT  # noqa: E402
from holo_cap import NONCE_CANDS  # noqa: E402
from holo_frag import _json_safe  # noqa: E402
from stack_error_domain import build_categories, classify, first_token  # noqa: E402

from verbum.dsp import gate, paired_permutation  # noqa: E402

OPERAND_DOMAIN = {"CITY", "COUNTRY", "CONTINENT"}
ENRICH_DEPTH = 0.16                    # d_ct build/injection depth (P-ENRICH-1)
CC_FRAME_HEAD = "The landmark is located in the country of"
PROSE_DONOR = "The recipe calls for two cups of white wheat flour"
SYNTH_DONOR_HEAD = "Consider the"
ARMS = ("base", "kv_nat", "kv_wrong", "kv_rand", "kv_synth", "resid")


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Frozen verdict logic (pure; --validate exercises it)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def score_kv(m: dict[str, np.ndarray], acc: dict[str, float],
             op_err: dict[str, np.ndarray], swap_hits: int, true_hits: int,
             rng, alpha: float) -> dict:
    def g(a, b, name):
        return gate(float(np.mean(m[a] - m[b])),
                    paired_permutation(m[a], m[b], rng), "greater", alpha,
                    name=name)
    g1 = g("kv_nat", "base", "address_works")            # primary
    flip = bool(acc["kv_nat"] > acc["base"])
    g2 = g("kv_nat", "kv_wrong", "specificity")
    g3 = g("kv_nat", "kv_rand", "not_any_kv")
    g4 = g("kv_synth", "resid", "register_fork")         # mechanism clause
    synth_flip = bool(acc["kv_synth"] > acc["resid"])
    swap_coherent = bool(swap_hits > true_hits)          # advisory, never gated
    sec = gate(float(np.mean(op_err["base"] - op_err["kv_nat"])),
               paired_permutation(op_err["base"], op_err["kv_nat"], rng),
               "greater", alpha, name="operand_err_shift")
    return {"g1": g1, "flip": flip, "g2": g2, "g3": g3, "g4": g4,
            "synth_flip": synth_flip, "swap_coherent": swap_coherent,
            "swap_hits": swap_hits, "true_hits": true_hits, "secondary": sec,
            "acc": dict(acc), "means": {a: float(np.mean(m[a])) for a in m}}


def verdict_kv(gate0: bool, sc: dict) -> str:
    if not gate0:
        return "negative/inconclusive (gate-0)"
    if not (sc["g1"].verdict and sc["flip"]):
        return "ADDRESS-FAILS"          # tape power != addressability
    if not sc["g3"].verdict:
        return "ANY-KV-ARTIFACT"
    if not sc["g2"].verdict:
        return "KV-PRIMING"
    if sc["g4"].verdict and sc["synth_flip"]:
        return "ADDRESSED-COMPOSES (+ADDRESS-SUFFICIENT)"
    return "ADDRESSED-COMPOSES (+RE-ENCODING-REQUIRED)"


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# --validate: planted worlds (no model)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_validate(alpha: float) -> int:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    n, noise = 10, 0.3
    print("── P-KV-1 --validate (planted worlds, no model) ──")
    ok = True

    def world(mu: dict[str, float], accs: dict[str, float], swap=(0, 0)):
        m = {a: mu.get(a, 0.0) + rng.normal(0, noise, n) for a in ARMS}
        op = {"base": np.array([1.0] * 8 + [0.0] * 2),
              "kv_nat": np.array([1.0] * 2 + [0.0] * 8)}
        acc = {a: accs.get(a, 0.0) for a in ARMS}
        sc = score_kv(m, acc, op, swap[0], swap[1], rng, alpha)
        return verdict_kv(True, sc)

    calls = {
        # address works, specific, not-any-kv; synth also beats resid -> SUFFICIENT
        "sufficient": (world({"kv_nat": 1.6, "kv_synth": 1.2, "kv_wrong": 0.2,
                              "kv_rand": 0.1, "resid": 0.1},
                             {"kv_nat": 0.7, "kv_synth": 0.4}),
                       "ADDRESSED-COMPOSES (+ADDRESS-SUFFICIENT)"),
        # address works for donor-encoded content only -> RE-ENCODING-REQUIRED
        "reencode": (world({"kv_nat": 1.6, "kv_wrong": 0.2, "kv_rand": 0.1,
                            "kv_synth": 0.15, "resid": 0.1},
                           {"kv_nat": 0.7}),
                     "ADDRESSED-COMPOSES (+RE-ENCODING-REQUIRED)"),
        # wrong-country KV moves margins just as much -> priming
        "priming": (world({"kv_nat": 1.6, "kv_wrong": 1.5, "kv_rand": 0.1},
                          {"kv_nat": 0.7}),
                    "KV-PRIMING"),
        # any attendable KV reproduces the gain -> artifact
        "any-kv": (world({"kv_nat": 1.6, "kv_wrong": 0.2, "kv_rand": 1.5},
                         {"kv_nat": 0.7}),
                   "ANY-KV-ARTIFACT"),
        # nothing moves -> fails
        "fails": (world({}, {}), "ADDRESS-FAILS"),
        # margins move, argmax does not -> fails
        "no-flip": (world({"kv_nat": 1.6, "kv_wrong": 0.2, "kv_rand": 0.1}, {}),
                    "ADDRESS-FAILS"),
    }
    for w, (call, want) in calls.items():
        good = call == want
        print(f"[V] {w}-world -> {call} (want {want}) {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}")
        ok &= good
    print(f"\n── --validate {'ALL PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} ──")
    return 0 if ok else 1


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Model path
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_model(args) -> int:
    import operand_multihop3 as mh3
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

    dev = (args.device if (args.device != "mps" or torch.backends.mps.is_available())
           else "cpu")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_id)
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        args.model_id, dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype),
        attn_implementation="eager").to(dev).eval()
    dec, _norm, _u = mh3.resolve_parts(model)
    n_layers = len(dec)
    L, S = args.ref_layer, args.scale
    L_e = round(ENRICH_DEPTH * n_layers)
    print(f"[kv1] {args.model_id} L_ref={L} L_enrich={L_e} scale={S} "
          f"kv_scale={args.enrich_scale} dev={dev} n_layers={n_layers} eager")

    nonce = NONCE_CANDS[0]
    nonce_tid = tok(" " + nonce, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[-1]

    def first_tid(w):
        return mh3.first_tid(tok, w)

    def ids_of(text):
        return tok(text, return_tensors="pt").input_ids[0].tolist()

    # ── 4D additive mask builder ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    NEG = torch.finfo(getattr(torch, args.dtype)).min

    def build_mask(d_len: int, t_len: int, sel_cols: list[int]):
        n = d_len + t_len
        m = torch.full((n, n), NEG)
        tri = torch.tril(torch.zeros(n, n) == 0)
        m[tri] = 0.0                                   # causal base
        # test rows: block ALL donor cols, then re-open sink + selected
        m[d_len:, :d_len] = NEG
        allow = [0, *sel_cols]
        for c in allow:
            m[d_len:, c] = 0.0
        return m[None, None, :, :].to(dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype),
                                      device=dev)

    # ── mask-path self-check (gate on instrument; abort on fail) ─────────────
    def forward_logits(ids_list, mask4d=None, hooks=()):
        ids = torch.tensor([ids_list], device=dev)
        handles = [dec[li].register_forward_hook(mh3.add_hook_at(v, p))
                   for (li, v, p) in hooks]
        with torch.no_grad():
            if mask4d is None:
                lo = model(input_ids=ids).logits
            else:
                lo = model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=mask4d).logits
        for h in handles:
            h.remove()
        return lo[0, -1, :].float().cpu().numpy()

    chk_ids = ids_of("The capital of Portugal is")
    nchk = len(chk_ids)
    full = torch.full((nchk, nchk), NEG)
    full[torch.tril(torch.zeros(nchk, nchk) == 0)] = 0.0
    full = full[None, None, :, :].to(dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype), device=dev)
    lo_plain = forward_logits(chk_ids)
    lo_mask = forward_logits(chk_ids, mask4d=full)
    dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(lo_plain - lo_mask)))
    same_arg = bool(np.argmax(lo_plain) == np.argmax(lo_mask))
    mask_ok = same_arg and dmax < args.mask_tol
    print(f"[kv1] mask self-check: max|dlogit|={dmax:.5f} argmax_same={same_arg} "
          f"-> {'PASS' if mask_ok else 'FAIL'}")
    if not mask_ok:
        print("[kv1] ABORT: 4D mask path does not reproduce plain forward "
              "(no verdict from an unverified mask path)")
        return 2

    # ── union / ceilings / cells (bake_stack convention) ─────────────────────
    cap_labels = sorted({COUNTRY_CAP[mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]] for lm in mh3.LM_LIST
                         if mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm] in COUNTRY_CAP})
    vocab = (set(mh3.CONTINENTS) | set(mh3.COUNTRIES) | set(mh3.CITIES)
             | set(cap_labels))
    tid_map, drop = {}, set()
    for w in sorted(vocab):
        t = first_tid(w)
        clash = [x for x, tt in tid_map.items() if tt == t]
        if clash:
            drop.add(w)
            drop.update(clash)
        tid_map[w] = t
    union = {w: tid_map[w] for w in sorted(vocab - drop)}
    print(f"[kv1] union candidates: {len(union)} (dropped: {sorted(drop)})")

    def real_pred(prefix, query, word, labels):
        ids = tok(prefix + query.format(x=word), return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
        with torch.no_grad():
            lo = model(**ids).logits[0, -1, :].float().cpu().numpy()
        return max(labels, key=lambda w: lo[first_tid(w)])

    pref = ("The capital of Portugal is Lisbon.\nThe capital of Japan is Tokyo.\n"
            "The capital of Kenya is Nairobi.\n")
    valid = []
    for lm in mh3.LM_LIST:
        c = mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]
        if c not in COUNTRY_CAP or mh3.CITY_OF[lm] == COUNTRY_CAP[c]:
            continue
        cap = COUNTRY_CAP[c]
        if first_token(real_pred(pref, "The capital of {x} is", c,
                                 list(COUNTRY_CAP.values()))) == first_token(cap):
            valid.append(lm)
    gate0 = mask_ok and len(valid) >= 6
    print(f"[kv1] ceilings: valid landmarks {len(valid)}/{len(mh3.LM_LIST)} "
          f"gate0={gate0}")
    if args.n_cells:
        valid = valid[:args.n_cells]

    # ── directions: operands @ L_ref, countries @ L_e (P-ENRICH convention) ──
    def build_dirs(items, cap_L):
        per = {e: [] for e in items}
        for fr in mh3.FRAMES:
            for e in items:
                store: dict[int, np.ndarray] = {}
                h = dec[cap_L].register_forward_hook(mh3.cap_hook(store, cap_L))
                ids = tok(fr.format(x=e), return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
                with torch.no_grad():
                    model(**ids)
                h.remove()
                per[e].append(store[cap_L][0, -2, :])
        em = {e: np.mean(per[e], axis=0) for e in items}
        gm = np.mean([em[e] for e in items], axis=0)
        return {e: em[e] - gm for e in items}

    d_lm = build_dirs(mh3.LM_LIST, L)
    test_countries = sorted({mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm] for lm in valid})
    d_ct = build_dirs(test_countries, L_e)

    def derange(cs):
        n = len(cs)
        return {cs[i]: cs[(i + 1) % n] for i in range(n)} if n > 1 else {}

    wrong_of = derange(test_countries)

    # ── donor construction (padded to fixed length; selection = tail tokens) ──
    pad_ids = tok(" and so on", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids

    def donor(text, n_sel):
        """Returns (ids, sel_cols) — sel = last n_sel content positions."""
        ids = ids_of(text)
        sel = list(range(len(ids) - n_sel, len(ids)))
        return ids, sel

    def pad_to(ids, d_fix):
        out = list(ids)
        while len(out) < d_fix:
            out.append(pad_ids[len(out) % len(pad_ids)])
        return out[:d_fix]

    def country_donor(c):
        n_ct = len(tok(" " + c, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids)
        return donor(f"{CC_FRAME_HEAD} {c}", n_ct)

    synth_text = f"{SYNTH_DONOR_HEAD} {nonce}"
    test_ids = ids_of(NONCE_PROMPT.format(x=nonce))
    occ = [i for i, t in enumerate(test_ids) if t == nonce_tid][-1]

    d_fix = max([len(country_donor(c)[0]) for c in test_countries]
                + [len(ids_of(PROSE_DONOR)), len(ids_of(synth_text))]) + 2
    print(f"[kv1] donor length (fixed): {d_fix} | test len: {len(test_ids)} "
          f"| test nonce abs pos: {d_fix} + {occ}")

    # ── per-cell arms ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def cell_logits(lm, arm, c, cw):
        n_ct = len(tok(" " + c, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids)
        if arm in ("base", "resid"):
            d_ids, sel = donor(PROSE_DONOR, 0)[0], []
        elif arm == "kv_nat":
            d_ids, sel = country_donor(c)
        elif arm == "kv_wrong":
            d_ids, sel = country_donor(cw)
        elif arm == "kv_rand":
            d_ids, sel = donor(PROSE_DONOR, n_ct)   # column-count matched
        elif arm == "kv_synth":
            d_ids, sel = donor(synth_text, 1)
        d_ids = pad_to(d_ids, d_fix)
        ids = d_ids + test_ids
        mask = build_mask(d_fix, len(test_ids), sel)
        hooks = [(L, torch.tensor(d_lm[lm] * S, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev),
                  d_fix + occ)]
        if arm == "kv_synth":
            hooks.append((L_e, torch.tensor(d_ct[c] * args.enrich_scale,
                                            dtype=torch.float32, device=dev),
                          sel[0]))
        if arm == "resid":
            hooks.append((L_e, torch.tensor(d_ct[c] * args.enrich_scale,
                                            dtype=torch.float32, device=dev),
                          d_fix + occ))
        return forward_logits(ids, mask4d=mask, hooks=hooks)

    def margin_true(lo, target):
        others = [t for w, t in union.items() if w != target]
        return float(lo[union[target]] - max(lo[t] for t in others))

    def argmax_word(lo):
        return max(union, key=lambda w: lo[union[w]])

    cells = [lm for lm in valid
             if COUNTRY_CAP[mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]] in union
             and mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm] in wrong_of]
    cats = build_categories([{"country": mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm],
                              "city": mh3.CITY_OF[lm]} for lm in cells])
    print(f"[kv1] cells: {len(cells)}")

    margins = {a: [] for a in ARMS}
    op_err = {a: [] for a in ("base", "kv_nat")}
    swap_hits = true_hits = 0
    records = []
    for lm in cells:
        c = mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]
        cw = wrong_of[c]
        truth = COUNTRY_CAP[c]
        swap_target = COUNTRY_CAP[cw]
        row = {"landmark": lm, "truth": truth, "country": c,
               "wrong_country": cw, "swap_target": swap_target,
               "city": mh3.CITY_OF[lm]}
        for a in ARMS:
            lo = cell_logits(lm, a, c, cw)
            margins[a].append(margin_true(lo, truth))
            aw = argmax_word(lo)
            row[f"{a}_arg"] = aw
            row[f"{a}_margin"] = margins[a][-1]
            if a in op_err:
                op_err[a].append(1.0 if classify(aw, truth, cats)
                                 in OPERAND_DOMAIN else 0.0)
            if a == "kv_wrong":
                if first_token(aw) == first_token(swap_target):
                    swap_hits += 1
                if first_token(aw) == first_token(truth):
                    true_hits += 1
        records.append(row)

    m_arr = {a: np.asarray(v) for a, v in margins.items()}
    acc = {a: float(np.mean([1.0 if classify(r[f"{a}_arg"], r["truth"], cats)
                             == "CORRECT" else 0.0 for r in records]))
           for a in ARMS}
    op_arr = {a: np.asarray(v) for a, v in op_err.items()}
    sc = score_kv(m_arr, acc, op_arr, swap_hits, true_hits, rng, args.alpha)
    v = verdict_kv(gate0, sc)

    print(f"[kv1] means: { {a: round(sc['means'][a], 3) for a in ARMS} }")
    print(f"[kv1] acc:   { {a: round(acc[a], 2) for a in ARMS} }")
    print(f"[kv1] G1 Δ={sc['g1'].value:+.3f} (p={sc['g1'].p:.4f}) "
          f"flip={sc['flip']} | G2 Δ={sc['g2'].value:+.3f} (p={sc['g2'].p:.4f}) "
          f"swap {swap_hits}/{len(cells)} vs true {true_hits} "
          f"| G3 Δ={sc['g3'].value:+.3f} (p={sc['g3'].p:.4f})")
    print(f"[kv1] G4 register fork Δ={sc['g4'].value:+.3f} (p={sc['g4'].p:.4f}) "
          f"synth_flip={sc['synth_flip']} | secondary "
          f"Δ={sc['secondary'].value:+.3f} (p={sc['secondary'].p:.4f})")
    print(f"[kv1] VERDICT: {v}")

    result = {
        "model_id": args.model_id, "probe": "P-KV-1", "seed": args.seed,
        "scale": S, "enrich_scale": args.enrich_scale, "ref_layer": L,
        "n_layers": n_layers, "enrich_layer": L_e, "donor_len": d_fix,
        "mask_check": {"max_dlogit": dmax, "argmax_same": same_arg},
        "alpha": args.alpha, "valid": valid, "union_size": len(union),
        "dropped_collisions": sorted(drop), "n_cells": len(cells),
        "gate0": gate0, "wrong_of": wrong_of,
        "gates": {"g1": asdict(sc["g1"]), "flip": sc["flip"],
                  "g2": asdict(sc["g2"]), "g3": asdict(sc["g3"]),
                  "g4": asdict(sc["g4"]), "synth_flip": sc["synth_flip"],
                  "secondary": asdict(sc["secondary"]),
                  "swap_hits": swap_hits, "true_hits": true_hits,
                  "swap_coherent": sc["swap_coherent"]},
        "means": sc["means"], "acc": acc, "verdict": v, "cells": records}
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out / "kv_splice.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(_json_safe(result), indent=2, allow_nan=False))
    print(f"[kv1] wrote {out}/kv_splice.json")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="P-KV-1 addressed-content KV splice")
    ap.add_argument("--validate", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--model-id", default="Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="mps")
    ap.add_argument("--dtype", default="bfloat16", choices=["float32", "bfloat16"])
    ap.add_argument("--ref-layer", type=int, default=9)
    ap.add_argument("--scale", type=float, default=2.0)
    ap.add_argument("--enrich-scale", type=float, default=2.0)
    ap.add_argument("--mask-tol", type=float, default=0.05)
    ap.add_argument("--n-cells", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=0.05)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="results/kv-splice/qwen3-4b")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.validate:
        return run_validate(args.alpha)
    return run_model(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

**Output:**
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""§P-KV-1b — the co-encoding term (kv_ctx).

Drafted s295 (Michael "let's proceed with a"). P-KV-1 measured address ⊕
re-encoding = 0.20 acc; CoT = 0.90. Candidate third term: CoT's intermediate
attends the QUESTION while being encoded; P-KV-1's donor was encoded blind.
Isolate that single term with a paired control at FIXED positions.

Layout (one forward, 4D mask, self-checked): A = question ("Consider the
{nonce}." — operand injected at nonce @ L_ref) → B = donor ("It is located in
the country of {x}", padded; with A visible, "It" binds the operand) → C =
readout (" The answer is").
  A rows: causal within A.  B rows: kv_ctx = attend A + causal-in-B;
  kv_blind = causal-in-B ONLY (same donor, same positions, encoded blind).
  C rows: all of A + selected B columns (country tokens) + causal-in-C.

Arms: ctx_base / kv_ctx / kv_blind / kv_ctx_wrong / kv_ctx_rand.
Gates: G1 co-encoding term (kv_ctx>kv_blind margin AND acc); G2
composition-in-layout (kv_ctx>ctx_base + flip); G3 specificity; advisory
yardstick rows (fraction of CoT anchor 0.90; kv_blind vs P-KV-1 kv_nat 0.20).
Verdicts: CO-ENCODING-LOADED / CO-ENCODING-NULL / UNSPECIFIC-CTX /
LAYOUT-BREAKS.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(_HERE) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HERE))

# reuse (no fork)
from fn_stack import COUNTRY_CAP  # noqa: E402
from holo_cap import NONCE_CANDS  # noqa: E402
from holo_frag import _json_safe  # noqa: E402
from kv_splice import OPERAND_DOMAIN, PROSE_DONOR  # noqa: E402
from stack_error_domain import build_categories, classify, first_token  # noqa: E402

from verbum.dsp import gate, paired_permutation  # noqa: E402

CTX_FRAME = "It is located in the country of"      # + " {country}"
A_TEXT = "Consider the {x}."
C_TEXT = " The answer is"
COT_ANCHOR = 0.90                                  # s294 native cot (advisory)
KVNAT_ANCHOR = 0.20                                # P-KV-1 kv_nat (advisory)
ARMS = ("ctx_base", "kv_ctx", "kv_blind", "kv_ctx_wrong", "kv_ctx_rand")


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Frozen verdict logic (pure; --validate exercises it)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def score_ctx(m: dict[str, np.ndarray], acc: dict[str, float],
              op_err: dict[str, np.ndarray], swap_hits: int, true_hits: int,
              rng, alpha: float) -> dict:
    def g(a, b, name):
        return gate(float(np.mean(m[a] - m[b])),
                    paired_permutation(m[a], m[b], rng), "greater", alpha,
                    name=name)
    g1 = g("kv_ctx", "kv_blind", "co_encoding_term")     # primary
    flip1 = bool(acc["kv_ctx"] > acc["kv_blind"])
    g2 = g("kv_ctx", "ctx_base", "composition_in_layout")
    flip2 = bool(acc["kv_ctx"] > acc["ctx_base"])
    g3 = g("kv_ctx", "kv_ctx_wrong", "specificity")
    swap_coherent = bool(swap_hits > true_hits)          # advisory
    sec = gate(float(np.mean(op_err["ctx_base"] - op_err["kv_ctx"])),
               paired_permutation(op_err["ctx_base"], op_err["kv_ctx"], rng),
               "greater", alpha, name="operand_err_shift")
    return {"g1": g1, "flip1": flip1, "g2": g2, "flip2": flip2, "g3": g3,
            "swap_coherent": swap_coherent, "swap_hits": swap_hits,
            "true_hits": true_hits, "secondary": sec, "acc": dict(acc),
            "cot_fraction": float(acc["kv_ctx"] / COT_ANCHOR),
            "means": {a: float(np.mean(m[a])) for a in m}}


def verdict_ctx(gate0: bool, sc: dict) -> str:
    if not gate0:
        return "negative/inconclusive (gate-0)"
    if not (sc["g2"].verdict and sc["flip2"]):
        return "LAYOUT-BREAKS"          # P-KV-1 effect lost in A-first layout
    if not (sc["g1"].verdict and sc["flip1"]):
        return "CO-ENCODING-NULL"       # address+re-encoding was the whole story
    if not sc["g3"].verdict:
        return "UNSPECIFIC-CTX"
    return "CO-ENCODING-LOADED"


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# --validate: planted worlds (no model)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_validate(alpha: float) -> int:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    n, noise = 10, 0.3
    print("── P-KV-1b --validate (planted worlds, no model) ──")
    ok = True

    def world(mu: dict[str, float], accs: dict[str, float], swap=(0, 0)):
        m = {a: mu.get(a, 0.0) + rng.normal(0, noise, n) for a in ARMS}
        op = {"ctx_base": np.array([1.0] * 8 + [0.0] * 2),
              "kv_ctx": np.array([1.0] * 2 + [0.0] * 8)}
        acc = {a: accs.get(a, 0.0) for a in ARMS}
        sc = score_ctx(m, acc, op, swap[0], swap[1], rng, alpha)
        return verdict_ctx(True, sc)

    calls = {
        # co-encoding adds a real term over blind, specific
        "loaded": (world({"kv_ctx": 2.4, "kv_blind": 1.2, "kv_ctx_wrong": 0.4,
                          "kv_ctx_rand": 0.2},
                         {"kv_ctx": 0.7, "kv_blind": 0.2}),
                   "CO-ENCODING-LOADED"),
        # ctx == blind: the address+re-encoding term was everything
        "null": (world({"kv_ctx": 1.2, "kv_blind": 1.2, "kv_ctx_wrong": 0.3},
                       {"kv_ctx": 0.2, "kv_blind": 0.2}),
                 "CO-ENCODING-NULL"),
        # ctx beats blind but wrong-country co-encodes just as well
        "unspecific": (world({"kv_ctx": 2.4, "kv_blind": 1.2,
                              "kv_ctx_wrong": 2.3},
                             {"kv_ctx": 0.7, "kv_blind": 0.2}),
                       "UNSPECIFIC-CTX"),
        # nothing composes in this layout at all
        "layout-breaks": (world({}, {}), "LAYOUT-BREAKS"),
        # margins move over blind but argmax does not -> NULL (flip1 required)
        "no-flip": (world({"kv_ctx": 2.4, "kv_blind": 1.2, "kv_ctx_wrong": 0.4},
                          {"kv_ctx": 0.3, "kv_blind": 0.3, "ctx_base": 0.0}),
                    "CO-ENCODING-NULL"),
    }
    for w, (call, want) in calls.items():
        good = call == want
        print(f"[V] {w}-world -> {call} (want {want}) {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}")
        ok &= good
    print(f"\n── --validate {'ALL PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} ──")
    return 0 if ok else 1


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Model path
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_model(args) -> int:
    import operand_multihop3 as mh3
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

    dev = (args.device if (args.device != "mps" or torch.backends.mps.is_available())
           else "cpu")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_id)
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        args.model_id, dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype),
        attn_implementation="eager").to(dev).eval()
    dec, _norm, _u = mh3.resolve_parts(model)
    n_layers = len(dec)
    L, S = args.ref_layer, args.scale
    print(f"[kvc] {args.model_id} L_ref={L} scale={S} dev={dev} "
          f"n_layers={n_layers} eager")

    nonce = NONCE_CANDS[0]
    nonce_tid = tok(" " + nonce, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[-1]

    def first_tid(w):
        return mh3.first_tid(tok, w)

    NEG = torch.finfo(getattr(torch, args.dtype)).min

    def forward_logits(ids_list, mask4d=None, hooks=()):
        ids = torch.tensor([ids_list], device=dev)
        handles = [dec[li].register_forward_hook(mh3.add_hook_at(v, p))
                   for (li, v, p) in hooks]
        with torch.no_grad():
            if mask4d is None:
                lo = model(input_ids=ids).logits
            else:
                lo = model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=mask4d).logits
        for h in handles:
            h.remove()
        return lo[0, -1, :].float().cpu().numpy()

    # ── mask self-check (gate on instrument) ─────────────────────────────────
    chk_ids = tok("The capital of Portugal is", return_tensors="pt"
                  ).input_ids[0].tolist()
    nchk = len(chk_ids)
    full = torch.full((nchk, nchk), NEG)
    full[torch.tril(torch.zeros(nchk, nchk) == 0)] = 0.0
    full = full[None, None, :, :].to(dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype), device=dev)
    lo_a, lo_b = forward_logits(chk_ids), forward_logits(chk_ids, mask4d=full)
    dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(lo_a - lo_b)))
    same_arg = bool(np.argmax(lo_a) == np.argmax(lo_b))
    mask_ok = same_arg and dmax < args.mask_tol
    print(f"[kvc] mask self-check: max|dlogit|={dmax:.5f} argmax_same={same_arg} "
          f"-> {'PASS' if mask_ok else 'FAIL'}")
    if not mask_ok:
        print("[kvc] ABORT: unverified mask path")
        return 2

    # ── union / ceilings (inherited) ─────────────────────────────────────────
    cap_labels = sorted({COUNTRY_CAP[mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]] for lm in mh3.LM_LIST
                         if mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm] in COUNTRY_CAP})
    vocab = (set(mh3.CONTINENTS) | set(mh3.COUNTRIES) | set(mh3.CITIES)
             | set(cap_labels))
    tid_map, drop = {}, set()
    for w in sorted(vocab):
        t = first_tid(w)
        clash = [x for x, tt in tid_map.items() if tt == t]
        if clash:
            drop.add(w)
            drop.update(clash)
        tid_map[w] = t
    union = {w: tid_map[w] for w in sorted(vocab - drop)}
    print(f"[kvc] union candidates: {len(union)} (dropped: {sorted(drop)})")

    def real_pred(prefix, query, word, labels):
        ids = tok(prefix + query.format(x=word), return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
        with torch.no_grad():
            lo = model(**ids).logits[0, -1, :].float().cpu().numpy()
        return max(labels, key=lambda w: lo[first_tid(w)])

    pref = ("The capital of Portugal is Lisbon.\nThe capital of Japan is Tokyo.\n"
            "The capital of Kenya is Nairobi.\n")
    valid = []
    for lm in mh3.LM_LIST:
        c = mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]
        if c not in COUNTRY_CAP or mh3.CITY_OF[lm] == COUNTRY_CAP[c]:
            continue
        if first_token(real_pred(pref, "The capital of {x} is", c,
                                 list(COUNTRY_CAP.values()))
                       ) == first_token(COUNTRY_CAP[c]):
            valid.append(lm)
    gate0 = mask_ok and len(valid) >= 6
    print(f"[kvc] ceilings: valid landmarks {len(valid)}/{len(mh3.LM_LIST)} "
          f"gate0={gate0}")
    if args.n_cells:
        valid = valid[:args.n_cells]

    # ── operand directions @ L_ref ───────────────────────────────────────────
    def build_dirs(items, cap_L):
        per = {e: [] for e in items}
        for fr in mh3.FRAMES:
            for e in items:
                store: dict[int, np.ndarray] = {}
                h = dec[cap_L].register_forward_hook(mh3.cap_hook(store, cap_L))
                ids = tok(fr.format(x=e), return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
                with torch.no_grad():
                    model(**ids)
                h.remove()
                per[e].append(store[cap_L][0, -2, :])
        em = {e: np.mean(per[e], axis=0) for e in items}
        gm = np.mean([em[e] for e in items], axis=0)
        return {e: em[e] - gm for e in items}

    d_lm = build_dirs(mh3.LM_LIST, L)
    test_countries = sorted({mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm] for lm in valid})

    def derange(cs):
        n = len(cs)
        return {cs[i]: cs[(i + 1) % n] for i in range(n)} if n > 1 else {}

    wrong_of = derange(test_countries)

    # ── segments ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    a_ids = tok(A_TEXT.format(x=nonce), return_tensors="pt").input_ids[0].tolist()
    c_ids = tok(C_TEXT, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    occ = [i for i, t in enumerate(a_ids) if t == nonce_tid][-1]
    pad_ids = tok(" and so on", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids

    def donor_ids(text, n_sel):
        ids = tok(" " + text, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
        sel_rel = list(range(len(ids) - n_sel, len(ids))) if n_sel else []
        return ids, sel_rel

    def pad_to(ids, b_fix):
        out = list(ids)
        while len(out) < b_fix:
            out.append(pad_ids[len(out) % len(pad_ids)])
        return out[:b_fix]

    def country_donor(c):
        n_ct = len(tok(" " + c, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids)
        return donor_ids(f"{CTX_FRAME} {c}", n_ct)

    b_fix = max([len(country_donor(c)[0]) for c in test_countries]
                + [len(donor_ids(PROSE_DONOR, 0)[0])]) + 2
    a_len, c_len = len(a_ids), len(c_ids)
    print(f"[kvc] A={a_len} B(fixed)={b_fix} C={c_len} | nonce pos {occ}")

    def build_mask(sel_abs: list[int], b_sees_a: bool):
        n = a_len + b_fix + c_len
        m = torch.full((n, n), NEG)
        m[torch.tril(torch.zeros(n, n) == 0)] = 0.0     # causal base
        b0, c0 = a_len, a_len + b_fix
        if not b_sees_a:
            m[b0:c0, :b0] = NEG                          # blind donor
        m[c0:, b0:c0] = NEG                              # C blocks B ...
        for col in sel_abs:
            m[c0:, col] = 0.0                            # ... except selected
        return m[None, None, :, :].to(dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype),
                                      device=dev)

    def cell_logits(lm, arm, c, cw):
        n_ct = len(tok(" " + c, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids)
        if arm == "ctx_base":
            b, sel_rel, sees = donor_ids(PROSE_DONOR, 0)[0], [], False
        elif arm == "kv_ctx":
            b, sel_rel = country_donor(c)
            sees = True
        elif arm == "kv_blind":
            b, sel_rel = country_donor(c)
            sees = False
        elif arm == "kv_ctx_wrong":
            b, sel_rel = country_donor(cw)
            sees = True
        elif arm == "kv_ctx_rand":
            b, sel_rel = donor_ids(PROSE_DONOR, n_ct)
            sees = True
        b = pad_to(b, b_fix)
        ids = a_ids + b + c_ids
        sel_abs = [a_len + r for r in sel_rel]
        mask = build_mask(sel_abs, sees)
        hooks = [(L, torch.tensor(d_lm[lm] * S, dtype=torch.float32,
                                  device=dev), occ)]
        return forward_logits(ids, mask4d=mask, hooks=hooks)

    def margin_true(lo, target):
        others = [t for w, t in union.items() if w != target]
        return float(lo[union[target]] - max(lo[t] for t in others))

    def argmax_word(lo):
        return max(union, key=lambda w: lo[union[w]])

    cells = [lm for lm in valid
             if COUNTRY_CAP[mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]] in union
             and mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm] in wrong_of]
    cats = build_categories([{"country": mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm],
                              "city": mh3.CITY_OF[lm]} for lm in cells])
    print(f"[kvc] cells: {len(cells)}")

    margins = {a: [] for a in ARMS}
    op_err = {a: [] for a in ("ctx_base", "kv_ctx")}
    swap_hits = true_hits = 0
    records = []
    for lm in cells:
        c = mh3.COUNTRY_OF[lm]
        cw = wrong_of[c]
        truth = COUNTRY_CAP[c]
        swap_target = COUNTRY_CAP[cw]
        row = {"landmark": lm, "truth": truth, "country": c,
               "wrong_country": cw, "swap_target": swap_target,
               "city": mh3.CITY_OF[lm]}
        for a in ARMS:
            lo = cell_logits(lm, a, c, cw)
            margins[a].append(margin_true(lo, truth))
            aw = argmax_word(lo)
            row[f"{a}_arg"] = aw
            row[f"{a}_margin"] = margins[a][-1]
            if a in op_err:
                op_err[a].append(1.0 if classify(aw, truth, cats)
                                 in OPERAND_DOMAIN else 0.0)
            if a == "kv_ctx_wrong":
                if first_token(aw) == first_token(swap_target):
                    swap_hits += 1
                if first_token(aw) == first_token(truth):
                    true_hits += 1
        records.append(row)

    m_arr = {a: np.asarray(v) for a, v in margins.items()}
    acc = {a: float(np.mean([1.0 if classify(r[f"{a}_arg"], r["truth"], cats)
                             == "CORRECT" else 0.0 for r in records]))
           for a in ARMS}
    op_arr = {a: np.asarray(v) for a, v in op_err.items()}
    sc = score_ctx(m_arr, acc, op_arr, swap_hits, true_hits, rng, args.alpha)
    v = verdict_ctx(gate0, sc)

    print(f"[kvc] means: { {a: round(sc['means'][a], 3) for a in ARMS} }")
    print(f"[kvc] acc:   { {a: round(acc[a], 2) for a in ARMS} }")
    print(f"[kvc] G1 co-encoding Δ={sc['g1'].value:+.3f} (p={sc['g1'].p:.4f}) "
          f"flip={sc['flip1']} | G2 Δ={sc['g2'].value:+.3f} "
          f"(p={sc['g2'].p:.4f}) flip={sc['flip2']} | G3 Δ={sc['g3'].value:+.3f} "
          f"(p={sc['g3'].p:.4f}) swap {swap_hits}/{len(cells)} vs true {true_hits}")
    print(f"[kvc] advisory: CoT fraction {sc['cot_fraction']:.2f} "
          f"(kv_ctx {acc['kv_ctx']:.2f}/{COT_ANCHOR}) | kv_blind {acc['kv_blind']:.2f} "
          f"vs P-KV-1 kv_nat {KVNAT_ANCHOR} | secondary "
          f"Δ={sc['secondary'].value:+.3f} (p={sc['secondary'].p:.4f})")
    print(f"[kvc] VERDICT: {v}")

    result = {
        "model_id": args.model_id, "probe": "P-KV-1b", "seed": args.seed,
        "scale": S, "ref_layer": L, "n_layers": n_layers,
        "a_len": a_len, "b_fix": b_fix, "c_len": c_len,
        "mask_check": {"max_dlogit": dmax, "argmax_same": same_arg},
        "alpha": args.alpha, "valid": valid, "union_size": len(union),
        "dropped_collisions": sorted(drop), "n_cells": len(cells),
        "gate0": gate0, "wrong_of": wrong_of,
        "gates": {"g1": asdict(sc["g1"]), "flip1": sc["flip1"],
                  "g2": asdict(sc["g2"]), "flip2": sc["flip2"],
                  "g3": asdict(sc["g3"]), "secondary": asdict(sc["secondary"]),
                  "swap_hits": swap_hits, "true_hits": true_hits,
                  "swap_coherent": sc["swap_coherent"]},
        "advisory": {"cot_fraction": sc["cot_fraction"],
                     "cot_anchor": COT_ANCHOR, "kvnat_anchor": KVNAT_ANCHOR},
        "means": sc["means"], "acc": acc, "verdict": v, "cells": records}
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out / "kv_ctx.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(_json_safe(result), indent=2, allow_nan=False))
    print(f"[kvc] wrote {out}/kv_ctx.json")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="P-KV-1b co-encoding term (kv_ctx)")
    ap.add_argument("--validate", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--model-id", default="Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="mps")
    ap.add_argument("--dtype", default="bfloat16", choices=["float32", "bfloat16"])
    ap.add_argument("--ref-layer", type=int, default=9)
    ap.add_argument("--scale", type=float, default=2.0)
    ap.add_argument("--mask-tol", type=float, default=0.05)
    ap.add_argument("--n-cells", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=0.05)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="results/kv-ctx/qwen3-4b")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.validate:
        return run_validate(args.alpha)
    return run_model(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def circular_ordering(points: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """PCA-2 the 7 anchors; fraction of days in correct cyclic order (best
    alignment over rotation × reflection). 1.0 = perfect circle order."""
    x = points - points.mean(axis=0)
    _, _, vt = np.linalg.svd(x, full_matrices=False)
    p2 = x @ vt[:2].T
    ang = np.arctan2(p2[:, 1], p2[:, 0])
    order = list(np.argsort(ang))
    best = 0
    n = len(order)
    for direction in (order, order[::-1]):
        for shift in range(n):
            seq = direction[shift:] + direction[:shift]
            best = max(best, sum(1 for i, d in enumerate(seq) if d == i))
    return best / n


def _wrap(a: float) -> float:
    return float((a + np.pi) % (2 * np.pi) - np.pi)


def capture_all(args) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    from trace import calibrate_register  # opcodes/

    import capture as C  # opcodes/ (sys.path)
    import topology as T  # opcodes/
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        args.model, dtype=torch.bfloat16, low_cpu_mem_usage=True).eval()
    if args.device != "cpu":
        model = model.to(args.device)
    topo = T.detect_topology(model, model.config)
    n_layers = topo.n_layers
    print(f"[isr] {args.model} | {topo.summary()}")

    meta: dict[str, Any] = {
        "probe": "P-ITERATED-SOFT-REDUCTION", "freeze_commit": "078af23f",
        "model": args.model, "device": args.device,
        "model_revision": getattr(model.config, "_commit_hash", None),
        "timestamp_utc": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "git_sha": subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=_ROOT,
                                  capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip(),
        "n_perm": args.n_perm, "z_thresh": args.z, "smoke": bool(args.smoke),
        "lib_versions": {"torch": torch.__version__,
                         "numpy": np.__version__},
        "sampling": "greedy/argmax readout only; no generation",
    }
    lin_items = build_lin_items()
    circ_items = build_circ_items()
    meta["corpus_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(
        [lin_items, circ_items], sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:8]

    data: dict[str, Any] = {"secondary": {}}

    # ---- D1: gate-register calibration + SY-share ------------------------
```

# API Map: `verbum` repo — for building `src/verbum/driver.py`

## 1. MODEL LOADING (Qwen3-14B on MPS)

**No shared loader helper exists.** Every harness (`route_map_v0.py`, `arith_trace.py`, `iterated_soft_reduction.py`, `opcodes/trace.py`) repeats the same ~6-line `transformers` load inline. There is no `ModelConfig`/registry for raw-torch loads — `src/verbum/probes/models.py` (`REGISTRY`, `ModelConfig` from `verbum.probes.harness`) is a **different, HTTP/llama.cpp registry** (chat/completion transport against local llama.cpp servers on `:5100+`), not applicable to direct HF/torch loading. **You should write the loader fresh in `driver.py`**, following this exact pattern (canonical across all scripts):

```python
# scripts/experiments/iterated_soft_reduction.py, scripts/explore/arith_trace.py, opcodes/trace.py
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    args.model, dtype=torch.bfloat16, low_cpu_mem_usage=True).eval()
if args.device != "cpu":
    model = model.to(args.device)
```

`scripts/experiments/dmd_transport.py` `class RealBackend` (used by `route_map_v0.py`) is the closest thing to a reusable wrapper — a "load once, expose model/tok/device" object:

```python
# scripts/experiments/dmd_transport.py:362-381
class RealBackend:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, device: str, dtype_str: str):
        dtype = getattr(torch, dtype_str)
        self.tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_id)
        self.model = (
            AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                model_id, torch_dtype=dtype, attn_implementation="eager"
            ).to(device).eval()
        )
        self.n_layers = len(find_gate_modules(self.model))
        self.d_model = int(self.model.config.hidden_size)
```
Note the parameter-name inconsistency in the repo: `dmd_transport.RealBackend` and `kv_splice.py`/`kv_ctx.py` use `dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype)` passed as `dtype=...` too (transformers accepts both `dtype=` and legacy `torch_dtype=`; either works with the installed version — pick `dtype=` to match the majority, e.g. `iterated_soft_reduction.py`, `arith_trace.py`).

- **Model id:** `"Qwen/Qwen3-14B"` — HF Hub id, **no local path**, used directly (default in `route_map_v0.py --model-id`, `iterated_soft_reduction.py --model`, `compile_step.py --model-id`).
- **dtype:** `bfloat16` (default everywhere; `route_map_v0.py --dtype` choices are `float32|float16|bfloat16`).
- **device_map:** none used — models are `.to(device)`'d manually (`"mps"` default), not `device_map="auto"`.
- **trust_remote_code:** **not used** for Qwen3 (only seen for unrelated older scripts, `scripts/probe_compression_v2.py`, `scripts/v8/tokenizer.py`).
- **revision pin:** **none** — no `revision=` argument anywhere for Qwen3-14B loads.
- **attn_implementation:** `"eager"` is used specifically where hooks/attention-mask surgery is needed (`dmd_transport.py`, `kv_splice.py`, `kv_ctx.py`); the opcode-trace scripts (`opcodes/trace.py`, `route_map_v0.py`, `arith_trace.py`) don't set it (default sdpa).
- **Provenance field for records:** `"model_revision": getattr(model.config, "_commit_hash", None)` (`iterated_soft_reduction.py:454`).

## 2. CAPTURE HOOKS (gate-preactivation)

Canonical home: **`opcodes/`** (top-level dir, not under `src/`) — `opcodes/{topology,capture,classify,trace,probes}.py`. Imported by inserting `opcodes/` onto `sys.path` (`sys.path.insert(0, str(_ROOT / "opcodes"))`).

### `opcodes/topology.py`
- `detect_topology(model, config=None) -> ModelTopology` — auto-detects the FFN routing register per-architecture (`gated-dense`/`gated-fused`/`ungated`/`moe`), locates `layers_path`, `gate_suffix` (e.g. `"mlp.gate_proj"`), `gate_width`, plus attention output path (`attn_suffix`, e.g. `"self_attn.o_proj"`).
- `gate_path(topo, layer) -> str` — dotted module path, e.g. `"model.layers.12.mlp.gate_proj"`.
- `ModelTopology.traceable` / `.validated_register` — gating properties.

### `opcodes/capture.py`
- `capture_gate(model, tokenizer, text=None, *, input_ids=None, topo=None, layers=None, register="gate") -> GateCapture` — **one forward pass**, hooks every (or selected) layer's `gate_proj` module (or the `attn` o_proj), returns:
```python
@dataclass
class GateCapture:
    gate: dict[int, np.ndarray]   # {layer_idx: ndarray[T, d]} — RAW gate_proj OUTPUT (pre-sign, all positions)
    input_ids: list[int]
    tokens: list[str]
    topo: ModelTopology
    register: str
```
- The captured tensor is the **`gate_proj` linear output** (i.e. the FFN gate pre-activation, before SiLU), **not** the residual hidden state. `capture_gate` does NOT take the sign or remove common-mode — that happens downstream in `classify.py`.
- Hook mechanics: `_module_for(i).register_forward_hook(_mk(i))`, storing `h[0]` (single-seq) → `store[i] = v.detach().float().cpu().numpy()`.
- Keyed by **layer index** (int), matching `topo.gate_path`/`layers` list.
- To read `sign(gate)`: `np.sign(cap.gate[layer_idx])` — done by the caller/classifier, e.g. in `route_map_v0.capture_signs`:
```python
# scripts/explore/route_map_v0.py:174 (a parallel, lighter-weight capture that
# directly signs at capture time via find_gate_modules, not opcodes/capture.py)
def hook(_m, _inp, out):
    buf[li] = np.sign(out[0, -1].detach().float().cpu().numpy()).astype(np.int8)
```

### `opcodes/classify.py`
- `RelationalCrystalClassifier(layers, n_perm=300, z_thresh=3.0, sil_z_thresh=2.0, seed=0, consensus_gram="auto", basis=None)` — the validated combinator-opcode reader.
  - `.calibrate(gate_by_layer, labels, null_gate_by_layer=None) -> dict[int, LayerCalib]` — computes `sign(gate)`, common-mode removal (`common = S.mean(axis=0)`, `X = S - common`), per-combinator centroids, null z-stats.
  - `.classify(gate_by_layer_token: dict[int, np.ndarray]) -> TokenOpcodes` — one token's per-layer gate vector → per-op z-scores; `TokenOpcodes.dominant` is the winning combinator label or `"·"` (no-op).
  - `CRYSTAL = ["K","I","B","C","S","D","W","Y","WHNF"]` (imported from `opcodes/vsm.py`).

### `opcodes/trace.py`
- `calibrate_register(model, tok, topo, register, layers, probes_per_comb, n_perm, z_thresh) -> (RelationalCrystalClassifier, summary_dict, (feat_np, labels_np, null_np))` — the canonical calibration entrypoint, reused verbatim by `arith_trace.py` (`from trace import calibrate_register`).
- `load(model_name, device)`, `trace_register(...)`, `main()` — full pipeline.

## 3. ROUTE/POLE FRAME (`route_map_v0.py`)

`scripts/explore/route_map_v0.py` — reuses **`combinator_relationship_map.find_gate_modules`** (regex-based, simpler than `opcodes/topology.py`):
```python
# scripts/experiments/combinator_relationship_map.py:108-116
def find_gate_modules(model):
    hits = []
    pat = re.compile(r"\.(\d+)\.mlp\.(gate_proj|dense_h_to_4h)$")
    for name, mod in model.named_modules():
        m = pat.search(name)
        if m:
            hits.append((int(m.group(1)), name, mod, m.group(2)))
    hits.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    return [(li, name, mod) for (li, name, mod, k) in hits]
```

**Pole basis:** `CRYSTAL9 = ["K","I","B","C","S","D","W","Y","WHNF"]`; `WHNF_STATES = [f"whnf:{o}" for o in [...7 non-Y ops...]]`; `BASIS17 = [*CRYSTAL9, *WHNF_STATES, "div:Y"]` (17 total, script's live pole frame).

**`results/expanded-gram/qwen3-14b/centroids.npz`** — confirmed keys (via direct load):
```
basis      (24,)  <U13   # 24-state basis labels (superset of BASIS17; route_map_v0 selects the 17-state subset via json instead, see below)
layers     (40,)  int64  # layer indices captured
centroids  (40, 24, 17408) float16  # [layer, basis_state, gate_width]
```
`route_map_v0.py` itself does **not read `centroids.npz` directly** — it reads `results/expanded-gram/qwen3-14b/expanded_gram.json` (`COMMITTED_XGRAM / "expanded_gram.json"`) for `basis` (24 labels) and `consensus_gram_24`, and instead **re-captures its own 17-basis pole frame from probes** (co-registration discipline — "the coext lesson: validate-plumbing == data-plumbing"), only comparing Gram alignment (`g0_coherence`) against the committed one.

**Cosine computation** (`route_map_v0.py`):
```python
def build_pole_frame(signs, states, order):
    """(P, mu): P[S,L,d] CMR'd UNIT pole centroids; mu[L,d] common mode."""
    st = np.array(states)
    cent = np.stack([signs[st == s].mean(axis=0) for s in order]).astype(np.float32)
    mu = cent.mean(axis=0)
    centc = cent - mu[None]
    nrm = np.linalg.norm(centc, axis=2, keepdims=True)
    P = centc / np.where(nrm < 1e-9, 1.0, nrm)
    return P, mu

def project_routes(signs, P, mu):
    """(n, L, S) cosine of each probe's CMR'd sign row onto each pole per layer."""
    x = signs.astype(np.float32) - mu[None]
    nrm = np.linalg.norm(x, axis=2, keepdims=True)
    xn = x / np.where(nrm < 1e-9, 1.0, nrm)
    return np.einsum("nld,sld->nls", xn, P).astype(np.float32)
```
Registers used: `sign(gate_proj)` last-token, **all layers**, via `capture_signs(be, texts, want, max_length)` (custom hook, sign taken at capture time — not via `opcodes/capture.py`).

## 4. OPCODE TRACER (`arith_trace.py`)

`scripts/explore/arith_trace.py` **reuses `opcodes/{trace,capture,topology,classify}` unmodified** — no re-implemented reader:
```python
sys.path.insert(0, str(_ROOT / "opcodes"))
from trace import calibrate_register       # canonical calibration
import capture as C
import topology as T
from classify import CRYSTAL
```
Flow:
1. `topo = T.detect_topology(model, model.config)`; refuse if `not topo.traceable`.
2. `rcc, summ, _ = calibrate_register(model, tok, topo, register, layers, probes_per_comb, n_perm, z_thresh)` — calibrates on the bundled crystal probes ONCE.
3. Per-token, per-layer trajectory: `cap = C.capture_gate(model, tok, prompt, topo=topo, layers=layers, register=register)`; for each position `res = rcc.classify({li: cap.gate[li][pos] for li in layers})`; take `op = max(zmap, key=zmap.get)` if `zmap[op] > z_thresh` else no-op.
4. `I_IDX = CRYSTAL.index("I")` — used to compute `I_frac` (fraction of firings that are the identity combinator).

This is the exact pattern your driver should follow for a live per-token combinator readout during generation.

## 5. KV / `past_key_values`

No generic KV-cache seal/fork utility exists as a reusable module. Direct API usage found in two places:

- **`scripts/experiments/lambda_machine.py`** — custom attention `forward` overrides that call the HF cache API directly:
```python
if past_key_values is not None:
    key_states, value_states = past_key_values.update(
        key_states, value_states, self_attn.layer_idx
    )
```
  (standard `Cache.update(key, value, layer_idx)` — this is the transformers `DynamicCache`/`Cache` protocol, used when monkey-patching `self_attn.forward` via `types.MethodType`.)

- **`scripts/explore/kv_splice.py`** (`P-KV-1`) and **`scripts/explore/kv_ctx.py`** (`P-KV-1b`) — do **not** use `past_key_values`/`DynamicCache` at all. Instead they build a **single forward pass** with a hand-built 4D additive attention mask (`torch.finfo(dtype).min` for blocked positions, `0.0` for allowed) to simulate "donor KV visible to test segment" without an actual cache object — donor + test tokens concatenated into one `input_ids`, and a `(1,1,n,n)` mask controls which positions can attend to which:
```python
def build_mask(d_len, t_len, sel_cols):
    n = d_len + t_len
    m = torch.full((n, n), NEG)
    m[torch.tril(torch.zeros(n,n)==0)] = 0.0     # causal base
    m[d_len:, :d_len] = NEG                        # block all donor cols from test rows
    for c in [0, *sel_cols]:
        m[d_len:, c] = 0.0                          # re-open sink + selected donor cols
    return m[None, None, :, :].to(dtype=..., device=dev)
```
Both scripts include a mandatory **mask self-check** (compare masked-forward logits vs plain forward logits, abort if they diverge) — the pattern to copy if `driver.py` forks/reuses cached KV via masking rather than the `Cache` object.

**Conclusion for your driver:** there is no existing "snapshot/fork `past_key_values`" helper. You'll be writing this from scratch; the one precedent (`lambda_machine.py`) shows the raw `Cache.update()` call signature, and `kv_splice.py`/`kv_ctx.py` show the mask-based alternative with its required self-check discipline.

## 6. LOGIT LENS

**`src/verbum/jlens.py`** — the canonical logit-lens module (package-importable, not opcodes/):
```python
@torch.no_grad()
def logit_lens(model: nn.Module, resid: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Map residual STATE(s) (..., d) to logits (..., vocab) (full norm)."""
    norm, unembed = _final_norm_unembed(model)
    dtype = next(model.parameters()).dtype
    dev = next(model.parameters()).device
    return unembed(norm(resid.to(dtype).to(dev)))

@torch.no_grad()
def verbalize(model, tokenizer, direction, *, top_k=8) -> list[str]:
    """Top-k tokens a residual DIRECTION points toward (no re-centering, gain-only)."""
    norm, unembed = _final_norm_unembed(model)
    d = direction.to(unembed.weight.dtype).to(unembed.weight.device)
    gain = getattr(norm, "weight", None)
    if gain is not None:
        d = d * gain.to(d.dtype)
    col = unembed.weight @ d
    idx = torch.topk(col, top_k).indices.tolist()
    return [tokenizer.decode([i]) for i in idx]
```
Helper `_final_norm_unembed(model)` resolves `(final_norm, lm_head)` per architecture (Qwen path: `model.model.norm`, `model.lm_head`). Also `capture_residuals(model, tokenizer, text=None, *, input_ids=None) -> (dict[int, Tensor], input_ids)` — full-layer residual capture via `verbum.hooks.capture`.

**`opcodes/jspace.py`** — parallel `verbalize_state`/`verbalize` used inside `opcodes/trace.py`'s `--operand` flag (J-space logit-lens "operand" column, `J.verbalize_state(model, tok, resids[read_layer][pos], topo=topo, top_k=top_k)`) — a topology-aware variant of the same idea, sidecar-only (never feeds the opcode classifier).

## 7. Package layout / invocation

**`src/verbum/__init__.py`** exports (public API surface):
```python
from verbum._protocol import CompletionResult, HealthStatus, ServerProps, StreamEvent, Timings
from verbum.client import AsyncClient, Client
from verbum.experiment import (CacheInterceptor, Computation, Context, Graph, Interceptor,
    LogInterceptor, ProvenanceInterceptor, ResourceInterceptor, default_interceptors)
from verbum.experiment import run as run_experiment
from verbum.probes import (Gate, Probe, ProbeSet, ResolvedProbe, gate_hash, load_gate,
    load_probe_set, probe_set_hash, resolve_probes)
from verbum.results import (ProbeRecord, Run, RunMeta, RunWriter, SamplingConfig,
    collect_provenance, content_hash, load_run)
from verbum.runner import RunSummary, fire_probe, run_probe_set
```
`jlens`, `hooks`, `instrument`, `cone`, `dsp/*`, `probes/library.py`, `probes/harness.py` etc. are importable as `verbum.jlens`, `verbum.hooks`, `verbum.probes.library`, etc. but **not re-exported at top level** — import them directly, e.g. `from verbum.probes.library import crystal_probes, all_probes` (used by `route_map_v0.py`).

Note: `opcodes/probes.py` also defines its own `crystal_probes()` (used by `opcodes/trace.py` via `from probes import crystal_probes` after `sys.path.insert`), distinct from `verbum.probes.library.crystal_probes`. Confirm which corpus you want if you build a driver that calibrates the classifier — both exist and are not identical objects.

**Invocation pattern:** `uv run python <script>.py --args` (per `opcodes/trace.py` docstring: `uv run python opcodes/trace.py --model Qwen/Qwen3-0.6B --smoke`). Level-1 deps (`torch`, `transformers`, `accelerate`, `safetensors`, `scikit-learn`) are in the `level1` **dependency group**, not core — install/run via `uv run --group level1 python ...` or ensure that group is synced. No `PYTHONPATH` env convention seen; scripts manipulate `sys.path` manually for peer-module imports (`opcodes/`, `scripts/experiments/`).

**`ipython`:** **not** a direct dependency. `pyproject.toml`'s `notebook` dependency-group lists `jupyterlab>=4.0`, `ipykernel>=6.29`, `matplotlib`, `seaborn` — `ipython` would only be pulled in transitively via `ipykernel`. If your driver needs a REPL, either target this `notebook` group (`uv sync --group notebook`) or add `ipython` explicitly as a new dependency.

With the map complete, I need to restack the queue by moving the §P-REPL-DRIVER row to the top with a re-scope prefix, then start building driver.py — using a RealBackend-style loader with eager attention implementation, bfloat16 on MPS, and a capture mechanism based on gate module detection.

For the driver itself, I'm thinking through how to wire persistent sign(gate) hooks per layer during generation, capturing the last token's gate sign per forward pass via a ring buffer per bounce. Since I also need residual hidden states for logit-lens analysis alongside the gate signs for pole cosine work, the cleanest approach is a manual step-by-step decode loop with output_hidden_states=True, which conveniently also gives me what I need for KV cache sealing and forking. For the pole frame itself, I'm weighing whether to re-capture from probes like route_map_v0 does, versus loading the already-committed centroid data across all 40 layers.

Now I'm working out the cheapest path: load the committed centroids npz, select a basis subset, apply CMR normalization, and unit-normalize to build the projection frame for sign vectors per layer — treating this as good enough for exploration while leaving proper co-registered recapture for later. For opcode classification, I'm deciding to make the RelationalCrystalClassifier calibration lazy since running crystal probes at load time costs several minutes. For KV seal/fork, I'm planning a manual decode loop using DynamicCache, where sealing means deep-copying the cache's key/value tensor lists cropped to length, and forking means resuming generation from that sealed snapshot.

I'm defining the correctness checks: forking with no alternative text and greedy decoding must exactly reproduce the original continuation, and resuming from a seal must match a full-prompt recompute token-for-token, confirming incremental KV matches full forward determinism. On memory, I'm estimating 14B bf16 at roughly 28GB with manageable KV seal sizes (tens of MB for a few hundred tokens), so I just need to cap the number of seals kept around.

Hidden states per token also stay manageable in size. I'm sketching the driver's init method to load the model, locate gate modules, and install hooks that capture sign values across all positions during prefill versus single positions during decode, storing them efficiently for later per-token analysis.

Then I'm designing the remaining methods: prefill to tokenize and cache signs for the full prompt, bounce to step through generation while recording signs and hidden states per new token, seal to snapshot cache state after a bounce, and fork to branch from a saved seal with alternate continuation text.

For the views, I'm planning routes to compute cosine similarities against a committed centroid frame, top_poles for pretty-printing those, lens to pull logit-lens projections from stored residuals via verbum.jlens, and opcodes to lazily calibrate against crystal probes by reusing the existing calibrate_register function with a sys.path tweak. I'm also designing fork-identity and append-law checks: comparing bounce results from a sealed prefill against a forked-with-empty-alt bounce, and verifying incremental cache-based generation matches a full recompute from the concatenated text. For attention read-mass, I'm deferring that since it needs eager attention with output_attentions enabled, though I could add a simple capture flag into the bounce step later.

Now I'm writing a cache-copying helper that clones the key/value tensors layer by layer into a new DynamicCache, while being defensive about API differences across transformers versions.

### Thinking

Now I'm starting on src/verbum/driver.py, planning to load the model with bfloat16 and eager attention, then figure out how to reuse the gate-module detection logic since it currently lives in a script rather than the importable package.

I'm working out the hook mechanism for capturing gate sign vectors during both prefill and decode, then sketching the manual decode loop using DynamicCache with seal/fork semantics via to_legacy_cache and from_legacy_cache, while checking version compatibility across recent transformers releases.

For storage, I'm planning to keep hidden states as float32 numpy arrays per token (manageable at ~840KB each) and figuring out how to extract attention mass per layer from the eager attention output during single-token decode steps.

I'm deciding to only capture attention during decode steps rather than prefill, since prefill attention scales quadratically with sequence length and would be too large unless explicitly requested. For the pole frame, I'm planning to lazily load committed centroids from an npz file and build a projection frame by selecting a basis subset, computing CMR across selected states per layer, and unit-normalizing to get manageable-sized projection matrices and mean vectors.

I'm working out the routing logic: given sign vectors, subtract the mean, normalize, then project via einsum to get per-layer basis coordinates. I'm figuring out which operations make up BASIS17 — it's CRYSTAL9 plus WHNF states for the non-Y operations, and reasoning through the counting (9 total ops minus Y minus WHNF itself leaves 7 non-Y ops: K, I, B, C, S, D, W) to confirm the WHNF_STATES list construction.

I'm confirming the count checks out: 9 + 7 + div:Y = 17, matching BASIS17. I'm also planning a safer approach that intersects requested basis elements with what's actually in the npz file and raises a clear error if anything's missing, plus sketching out the lens functionality to decode top tokens from stored hidden states, and an opcode classifier that lazily imports topology/trace modules and calibrates per-token labels.

I'm working through the calibrate_register signature and how classify() expects raw gate vectors, but realizing my hooks only store signs rather than raw values — since sign(sign(g)) is idempotent this might still work, though I need to check how common-mode removal interacts with that.

Now I'm sketching out the validity checks, starting with determinism: running bounce twice on the same prompt and confirming token ids and sign arrays match exactly.

For fork-identity, I want to verify that prefilling then sealing, then continuing versus forking with an empty string, produces identical token sequences, and that forking twice also agrees.

For the append law, I'm realizing retokenizing decoded text risks token healing mismatches, so instead I should compare at the id level — running one forward pass over the concatenated prompt and generated ids and checking argmax logits at each generated position against the original generation.

Since incremental and full forward passes have different tensor shapes, small bf16 numeric deviations are expected, so I'll gate the append-law check on argmax id equality rather than exact logit match, while still reporting the logit deviation as informational. Fork-identity, by contrast, uses identical shapes so I expect exact equality with zero deviation.

For the chat template option, I'm deciding to wrap text with apply_chat_template when chat=True, defaulting enable_thinking=False for Qwen3 to keep things compact, with params passed through simply. Now I'm sketching the Driver class API for the REPL, with an init taking model_id, device, dtype, and pole_frame, plus prefill and bounce methods that return Seal and Bounce objects respectively.

Continuing the API design, I'm adding fork for branching from a seal with alternate text, seal_of to get the seal at a bounce's end, routes to extract pole arrays, stations for a per-token argmax pole summary across layer bands, lens for top-k token views at a given step/layer, read_mass for token mass readouts, and calibrate_opcodes plus opcodes methods for the calibration workflow.

I'm also sketching the Bounce dataclass itself, holding prompt/new token ids, decoded tokens and text, sign and hidden state arrays, attention data, and a way to snapshot the final cache as an end_seal without doubling memory usage by keeping only the last cache internally.

I need a helper that prefills new ids into the cache then runs n greedy decode steps, relying on the cache length to handle position/rope automatically rather than managing attention masks manually. For tokenization I'll just use the tokenizer directly without worrying about BOS since Qwen3 doesn't add one by default, and I should consider whether to stop early on EOS generation.

Now I'm thinking through memory: capturing bf16 activation signs per token across all layers works out to roughly 700KB per token, so even long prompts with full prefill sign capture stay manageable at a few hundred MB, and I'm ready to write the actual file with a clean docstring and usage example.

I need to gate the hook capture with a flag so it only fires during intentional forward passes, not every validation pass, and have hooks append sign tensors per layer into buffers that get cleared and reassembled by the run method. For prefill chunks I'll concatenate frames across positions then stack into a tensor shaped by token count, layer, and hidden dim, while attention outputs can come directly from output_attentions without needing hooks, and I still need to decide whether to store hidden states for every position or just the last one during prefill.

Memory is the real constraint — storing full hidden states per token across all layers would balloon to hundreds of megabytes for a long prompt, so I'll store them as float16 and only capture hidden states by default during decode steps, not during prefill, unless explicitly requested.

Signs have the same problem: capturing them for every position at every layer would also blow up memory, so by default I'll only store the sign of the last token per forward pass, with an opt-in flag to capture all positions when needed.

Now I'm writing the Driver class itself — a REPL-friendly wrapper that loads the model once and stays resident, with methods for validity checks, prefill, bounce (greedy continuation with captures), and inspecting per-token route cosines.

I'm implementing the forward pass helper that runs the model with caching and optional hidden states/attentions, then working out EOS detection logic (handling both single and list-valued eos token ids) and tokenization for the fork method's alternate text branch.

Now I'm realizing an edge case: when forking with empty alt_text, there's no chunk to forward, so I need to decode directly from the sealed cache. The tricky part is that the cache holds keys/values for all processed tokens, but I still need the logits from the last position to pick the first continuation token, which means the Seal must store those last logits or the next input token explicitly.

I'm settling on storing `ids` and `logits_last` in the Seal, so continuation picks the greedy argmax from those logits and forks simply forward the alternate ids to get fresh logits. The key constraint is that forwarding mutates the cache in place, so continuing from a seal must clone the cache first rather than mutating the original.

Cloning the cache on every bounce keeps the seal immutable, and since each run works off its own cloned or pristine cache there's no risk of cross-contamination between bounces. I'm also setting up a registry dict mapping seal ids to Seal objects so prefill and end-of-run seals get auto-registered with labels for easy introspection later.

Now I'm designing validation checks: running two bounces from the same prompt to confirm deterministic output (matching ids, bounded logit deviation), then comparing a fresh bounce against one forked from a prefilled seal to verify they produce identical continuations — establishing that forking and direct bounce are computationally equivalent. I'm also checking an append law by teacher-forcing a fresh pass over the full sequence and confirming the argmax at each position matches the recorded continuation token, which validates that the cache-based generation is consistent with a cache-free forward pass.

I'm also sketching out attention mass extraction (averaging per-layer attention over heads at each decoding step) and an opcode classification system that maps each token's per-layer sign pattern against calibrated reference vectors to detect a dominant "opcode" when the z-score exceeds a threshold, plus a calibration routine that loads topology detection and registers layer-wise reference patterns for this classification.

I'll lazy-load the basis in routes, selecting the BASIS17 subset by name from the 24 labels and erroring if any are missing. The centroid array needs mean and normalized projection computed per selected state, with layer indices aligned to the 0-39 gate hook ordering so the sign array matches up correctly. No pyproject changes needed since ipython runs via --with; I should check the repo's ruff config for line-length conventions before finalizing.

Now I'm defining the Seal and Bounce dataclasses to hold generation state — Seal tracks token ids, decoded text, the cache object, and last logits, while Bounce captures the prompt, newly generated tokens, decoded text, and the sign array of gate_proj activations at each emitted position.

I need to be careful about the attention and hidden state arrays since they're ragged across steps, so I'll store them as lists of arrays rather than a single stacked array. I'm also working through the position semantics carefully — each forward step takes the previous token as input and produces logits for the next, so I need to make sure the captured signs correspond to the correct token's representation rather than the token being predicted.

I'll settle on defining signs[k], hidden[k], and attn[k] as the state at the position that emitted token k — so frame k comes from the forward pass whose logits chose new_ids[k], giving a clean "read-head" semantics of what was attended to right before producing that token. This makes attn[k] the query pattern over the full tape at the moment of emission, with frame 0 corresponding to the chunk's final prompt position.

For implementation, I'll collect hidden states and attentions from the chunk forward by taking the last position only (mean over heads for attention), then do the same for each step forward. I'll drop the unnecessary signs_all artifact to keep things minimal, then move to writing the code.

### Thinking

Starting the build now. I've settled on the semantics: captures align to the emitting position, so signs[k]/hidden[k]/attn[k] reflect the machine state at the forward pass that chose token k — matching what §P-READ-HEAD requires.
